    56	/**
    57	 * The heart on a real device, driven the way the side button drives it (#161, REF-09).
    58	 *
    59	 * PRODUCT OUTCOME rows: [aSideButtonTakeLandsInTheFocusedEditorExactlyOnce] (when it fails, the user's
    60	 * words did not reach the field they were typing in, or reached it twice), [aTakeStartedInAAndFocusMovedToBInsertsNowhereAndKeepsTheWordsOnTheClipboard]
    61	 * (the words reached a field the user had left, or the wrong field), [aDictationWithNoFieldToInsertIntoIsNotReportedToTheUserAsAFailure]
    62	 * (an ordinary tile dictation is reported as broken), and [transcribesThenPolishesWithSavedCustomWords]
    63	 * (the polish limb drops the user's own spellings).
    64	 *
    65	 * Every wait here is on a signal the SUBJECT fires, never elapsed time: the session owner's phase writes
    66	 * (`DictationSurfaceState`, read through an `OnSharedPreferenceChangeListener`), the History row the
    67	 * owner finalises (`TranscriptDao.observeAll`), the driver's audio-done broadcast, the rig's ordered
    68	 * focus answer, and `AudioTrack.onMarkerReached` for speaker playback. The one poll left is the polish
    69	 * row's wait on `isLocalModelReady`, a binder property with no completion callback, stated here rather
    70	 * than hidden.
    71	 *
    72	 * AUDIO ARRIVES FROM OUTSIDE. `-e audio external` (the default): the row publishes `driver_phase=READY`
    73	 * on the instrumentation stream with the reserved code [DRIVER_READY_STATUS] once it has seen LISTENING
    74	 * and finished staging; the driver (`scripts/uat/wispr_eyes.py` `run_device_test`) then feeds the audio
    75	 * and sends [ACTION_EXTERNAL_AUDIO_DONE] carrying this run's `-e driver_token`; the row waits for it, then
    76	 * stops the take. `-e audio speaker` plays the cached fixture through the phone's speaker instead, for a
    77	 * phone with no driver present. The editor's text is the rig's own receipt file, read from the test
    78	 * package's storage with `run-as`, an oracle this process did not write.
    79	 */

    81	class VoicePipelineDeviceTest {
    82	    companion object {

    95

    97	         * The WHOLE text the editor must hold after the driver's default sentence, a literal: the speech
    98	         * engine's own terminal period and the insertion's trailing space at the end of an empty field
    99	         * (`InsertionText`). Never derived from the sentence. `-e expected_final` overrides it, and the
   100	         * speaker path REQUIRES it because the cached fixture's words are the caller's.
   101	         */
   102	        const val EXPECTED_TEXT = "The quarterly marker sentence lands tomorrow. "
   103	        const val TEST_PACKAGE = "com.envi.wispr.test"
   104	
   105	        /** The owner's terminal statuses for a take that produced words: inserted, or kept on the clipboard. */

   107	    }
   108	
   109	    private val context = ApplicationProvider.getApplicationContext<Context>()
   110	    private val arguments: Bundle get() = InstrumentationRegistry.getArguments()
   111	    private val fixturePath = File(context.cacheDir, "enviouswispr-uat.pcm").path
   112	
   113	    // ---- the polish limb ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------
   114	
   115	    @Test
   116	    fun transcribesThenPolishesWithSavedCustomWords() {
   117	        assumeTrue("Physical-phone fixture is missing", File(fixturePath).isFile)
   118	        val (asr, asrConnection) = bind<IAsrService>(AsrService::class.java) { IAsrService.Stub.asInterface(it) }
   119	        val (polish, polishConnection) = bind<IPolishService>(PolishService::class.java) { IPolishService.Stub.asInterface(it) }
   120	        try {
   121	            polish.warmUpWithPolicy(PolishPolicy.LocalS1(S1ControlSettings.DEFAULT))
   122	            // A binder property with no completion callback: the one poll in this file, bounded.
   123	            val deadline = System.currentTimeMillis() + 30_000
   124	            while (!polish.isLocalModelReady && System.currentTimeMillis() < deadline) Thread.sleep(250)
   125	            assertTrue("S1-mini did not become ready: ${polish.localModelStatus()}", polish.isLocalModelReady)
   126	
   127	            val terms = BuiltinVocabulary.withUserTerms(
   128	                runBlocking { CustomTermRepository(context).list() }.map(CustomTermRecord::term),
   129	            )
   130	            val matcher = StructuredTermRestorer.compile(terms)
   131	
   132	            val asrFinished = CountDownLatch(1)
   133	            var rawText = ""
   134	            var asrError = ""
   135	            asr.transcribeFile(fixturePath, object : IAsrCallback.Stub() {
   136	                override fun onResult(text: String?) {
   137	                    rawText = text.orEmpty()
   138	                    asrFinished.countDown()
   139	                }
   140	
   141	                override fun onError(message: String?) {
   142	                    asrError = message.orEmpty()
   143	                    asrFinished.countDown()
   144	                }
   145	
   146	                // Never answered on the legacy request (#176): the legacy transaction keeps onError.
   147	                override fun onFailure(reason: Int, detail: String?) {
   148	                    asrError = "typed failure $reason on a legacy request"
   149	                    asrFinished.countDown()
   150	                }
   151	            })
   152	            assertTrue("ASR callback timed out", asrFinished.await(30, TimeUnit.SECONDS))
   153	            assertTrue("ASR returned no text: $asrError", rawText.isNotBlank())
   154	
   155	            val polishFinished = CountDownLatch(1)
   156	            var polishedText = ""
   157	            var engine = ""
   158	            var latencyMs = -1L
   159	            polish.polishRequest(1L, matcher.restore(rawText), true, true, false, PolishPolicy.LocalS1(S1ControlSettings.DEFAULT), object : IPolishCallback.Stub() {
   160	                override fun onOutcome(outcome: PolishOutcome?) {
   161	                    polishedText = matcher.restore(outcome?.text.orEmpty())
   162	                    engine = outcome?.engine.orEmpty()
   163	                    latencyMs = outcome?.latencyMs ?: -1L
   164	                    polishFinished.countDown()
   165	                }
   166	
   167	                override fun onResult(text: String?, usedEngine: String?, measuredLatencyMs: Long) = Unit
   168	
   169	                override fun onError(message: String?) = Unit
   170	            })
   171	            assertTrue("Polish callback timed out", polishFinished.await(30, TimeUnit.SECONDS))
   172	            assertTrue("Unexpected engine: $engine", engine.startsWith("S1-mini by Superwhisper"))
   173	            assertTrue("Saved product spelling was not applied: $polishedText", polishedText.contains("EnviousWispr"))
   174	            assertTrue("Saved name spelling was not applied: $polishedText", polishedText.contains("Saurabh"))
   175	            DebugLogger.log(
   176	                "VoicePipelineDeviceTest",
   177	                "rawChars=${rawText.length} engine=$engine latencyMs=$latencyMs polishedChars=${polishedText.length}"
   178	            )
   179	        } finally {
   180	            runCatching { context.unbindService(asrConnection) }
   181	            runCatching { context.unbindService(polishConnection) }
   182	        }
   183	    }
   184	
   185	    // ---- the heart, driven like the side button ----------------------------------------------------------
   186	
   187	    /**
   188	     * REVERT: write the text twice, or disable the editor write (the row fails even though the owner's
   189	     * log lines remain), or judge success from the caret.
   190	     */
   191	    @Test
   192	    fun aSideButtonTakeLandsInTheFocusedEditorExactlyOnce() {
   193	        val expected = expectedFinal()
   194	        val run = SideButtonRun()
   195	        startRig(twoFields = false)
   196	        run.recordOneTake(stage = {})
   197	        val row = run.awaitFinalRow()
   198	        val text = receipt(PasteTargetActivity.RECEIPT_NAME)
   199	        assertEquals("the editor's whole text is the literal expectation", expected, text)
   200	        assertEquals("the words appear exactly once", 1, occurrences(text, expected.trim()))
   201	        assertEquals("the owner recorded the commit route", InsertionResults.COMMITTED, row.insertionResult)
   202	        DebugLogger.log("VoicePipelineDeviceTest", "sideButtonTake chars=${text.length} result=${row.insertionResult}")
   203	    }
   204	
   205	    /**
   206	     * REF-01's two-editor case, asserting the product's FAIL-SAFE (`architecture-rules.md` RULE:
   207	     * insertion-fails-safe-never-silently): the take is pinned to editor A at its start; when B holds
   208	     * focus at insertion time the words go into NEITHER editor and stay on the clipboard, the paste
   209	     * service having waited for the original editor to return and given up (the live run 2026-09-22 read
   210	     * `outcome=NEVER_RETURNED attempts=20`, row `insertion_interrupted`/`copy_only`). A first draft of
   211	     * this row expected the words in A; the rig said otherwise and the product's rule is the authority.
   212	     * REVERT: insert into whatever is focused at insertion time (B gains the text), or write into an
   213	     * unfocused A.
   214	     */
   215	    @Test
   216	    fun aTakeStartedInAAndFocusMovedToBInsertsNowhereAndKeepsTheWordsOnTheClipboard() {
   217	        val run = SideButtonRun()
   218	        startRig(twoFields = true)
   219	        run.recordOneTake(stage = { moveFocusToB() })
   220	        val row = run.awaitFinalRow()
   221	        val a = receipt(PasteTargetActivity.RECEIPT_NAME)
   222	        val b = receipt(PasteTargetActivity.RECEIPT_B_NAME)
   223	        assertEquals("editor A, focused at the start but not at insertion, received nothing", "", a)
   224	        assertEquals("editor B, focused at insertion time, received nothing", "", b)
   225	        assertEquals("the owner recorded the copy-only fallback", InsertionResults.COPY_ONLY, row.insertionResult)
   226	        assertEquals("the row is closed as insertion interrupted", TranscriptEntity.STATUS_INSERTION_INTERRUPTED, row.status)
   227	    }
   228	
   229	    /**
   230	     * PRODUCT OUTCOME. When this fails, every dictation from the Quick Settings tile, the app's
   231	     * microphone button and the side button pressed outside a text field ends in a failure haptic, a
   232	     * long toast and a shade notification saying auto-paste did not reach the field. Those entry
   233	     * points cannot pin a target, so that is ordinary use of the product being reported as broken.
   234	     *
   235	     * Stages the alive-but-nothing-pinned case: only our own launcher is on screen, and the paste
   236	     * service excludes our own package except the onboarding practice box, which is not admitted
   237	     * here (`OwnFieldAdmission`), so nothing is pinned and the handoff is NO_PINNED_TARGET while the
   238	     * service is perfectly alive. There was no field, so the clipboard is the designed destination and
   239	     * the dictation SUCCEEDED.
   240	     *
   241	     * HONEST LIMIT: this proves the SILENCE on the no-field path, not the announcement on the
   242	     * SERVICE_NOT_RUNNING path. That cause cannot be staged in-process without killing the
   243	     * instrumentation or opening the private liveness field, which `validation-discipline.md`
   244	     * RULE: a-test-seam-on-a-GUARD-is-a-bypass forbids. The announcement is the adb recipe in
   245	     * `.claude/knowledge/device-testing.md`, whose step 6 dictates with the service unbound.
   246	     *
   247	     * The precondition is CONFIRMED after the run rather than assumed, per `validation-discipline.md`
   248	     * RULE: verify-the-feature-not-the-crash. `pinTarget` falls back to scanning every window, so a
   249	     * third-party editable field still focused behind our 1x1 launcher pins successfully and the
   250	     * dictation reaches the editor. Reporting that as a red row would accuse working code, so it
   251	     * reports SKIPPED with the observed handoff named. [observedHandoff] is a log read and stays a
   252	     * PRECONDITION read (it classifies the staging); it never judges the outcome.
   253	     */
   254	    @Test
   255	    fun aDictationWithNoFieldToInsertIntoIsNotReportedToTheUserAsAFailure() {
   256	        assumeTrue(
   257	            "Notifications are not permitted, so an announcement could not be posted either way " +
   258	                "and this row could not tell silence from a blocked notification",
   259	            NotificationManagerCompat.from(context).areNotificationsEnabled(),
   260	        )
   261	        assumeTrue(
   262	            "The accessibility service is not enabled",
   263	            shell("settings get secure enabled_accessibility_services")
   264	                .contains("com.envi.wispr/com.envi.wispr.paste.PasteAccessibilityService"),
   265	        )
   266	        val notificationManager = context.getSystemService(NotificationManager::class.java)
   267	        // The session's own notification is dismissed when the session ends, so an empty tray at
   268	        // the end of this run is the state to assert against.
   269	        notificationManager.cancel(DictationNotificationController.NOTIFICATION_ID)
   270	        // The ring buffer wraps, so observedHandoff() must read THIS run
   271	        // (`android-tooling.md` RULE: clear-logcat-before-you-measure).
   272	        shell("logcat -c")
   273	
   274	        val run = SideButtonRun()
   275	        run.recordOneTake(stage = {})
   276	        assertTrue("The session never returned to IDLE; phase was ${run.phaseNow()}", run.awaitIdle())
   277	
   278	        // The session owner names its own handoff, so the staged precondition is READ rather than
   279	        // assumed. Anything but NO_PINNED_TARGET means the phone was not in the state this row is
   280	        // about.
   281	        val handoff = observedHandoff()
   282	        assumeTrue(
   283	            "Nothing could be staged: the dictation reported handoff=$handoff, so an editable " +
   284	                "field was focused behind the launcher and the words went where they should",
   285	            handoff == "NO_PINNED_TARGET",
   286	        )
   287	
   288	        // NOT a check for one id. The durable fallback notification was DELETED in the messaging
   289	        // rework, so asserting its absence would be a green row about nothing
   290	        // (`testing-philosophy.md` RULE: the-rig-decides-where-a-test-lives-not-the-subject). What
   291	        // is asserted instead is the property that outlives it: after an ordinary dictation with no
   292	        // field, this app has left NOTHING in the user's shade. That goes red if any durable
   293	        // announcement comes back, whatever id it chooses.
   294	        // Two entries are NOT delivery announcements and are set aside by name: the polish notice
   295	        // (`POLISH_NOTIFICATION_ID`, "AI cleanup skipped" on a device whose local model is not ready,
   296	        // which is about the polish limb and is exactly what the emulator shows), and the SYSTEM's own
   297	        // silent-section group summary (id 1, tag `g:Aggregate_SilentSection`, `FLAG_GROUP_SUMMARY`),
   298	        // which the OS creates around any silent notification of ours. Everything else of ours is the
   299	        // property under test.
   300	        val ourNotifications = notificationManager.activeNotifications
   301	            .filter { it.packageName == context.packageName }
   302	            .filter { it.id != DictationNotificationController.POLISH_NOTIFICATION_ID }
   303	            .filter { (it.notification.flags and android.app.Notification.FLAG_GROUP_SUMMARY) == 0 }
   304	        val titles = ourNotifications.joinToString {
   305	            it.notification.extras?.getCharSequence(android.app.Notification.EXTRA_TITLE)
   306	                ?.toString()
   307	                .orEmpty() + " (id=${it.id})"
   308	        }
   309	        assertEquals(
   310	            "A dictation with no field to insert into left something in the shade: $titles. There " +
   311	                "was no field, the clipboard is the designed destination, and this is what every " +
   312	                "tile dictation and every in-app microphone-button dictation does.",
   313	            emptyList<String>(),
   314	            ourNotifications.map { "id=${'$'}{it.id}" },
   315	        )
   316	        DebugLogger.log("VoiceFallbackDeviceTest", "handoff=$handoff shadeAfterDictation=empty")
   317	    }
   318	
   319	    // ---- one side-button take, every wait on a signal -----------------------------------------------------
   320	
   321	    /**
   322	     * Owns one take: start the recorder like the side button, await the owner's LISTENING, run the row's
   323	     * staging, get the audio in (driver or speaker), stop, and hand back the History row the owner
   324	     * finalised. `startedAtMs` bounds the row wait so an earlier take's row cannot answer (the Stale edge).
   325	     */
   326	    private inner class SideButtonRun {
   327	        val startedAtMs = System.currentTimeMillis()
   328	        private val listening = CountDownLatch(1)
   329	        private val idle = CountDownLatch(1)
   330	        private val stopRequested = AtomicBoolean(false)
   331	        private val surfaceState = context.getSharedPreferences(SURFACE_STATE_PREFERENCES, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
   332	        private val phaseListener = SharedPreferences.OnSharedPreferenceChangeListener { store, key ->
   333	            if (key != SURFACE_STATE_PHASE) return@OnSharedPreferenceChangeListener
   334	            when (store.getString(key, null)) {
   335	                "LISTENING" -> listening.countDown()
   336	                "IDLE" -> if (stopRequested.get()) idle.countDown()
   337	            }
   338	        }
   339	
   340	        fun phaseNow(): String? = surfaceState.getString(SURFACE_STATE_PHASE, null)
   341	
   342	        fun recordOneTake(stage: () -> Unit) {
   343	            surfaceState.registerOnSharedPreferenceChangeListener(phaseListener)
   344	            val audio = audioForThisRun()
   345	            try {
   346	                audio.prepare()
   347	                // Instrumentation restarts the app's process, which kills the accessibility service; the
   348	                // system (or the driver) rebinds it, and the take must not start before it is BOUND, read
   349	                // from the service's own liveness fact (`PasteAccessibilityService.isBound`, subject-fired).
   350	                val bound = runBlocking { withTimeoutOrNull(30_000) { PasteAccessibilityService.isBound.first { it } } }
   351	                assertTrue(
   352	                    "the accessibility service is not bound, so no insertion can be judged (staging: " +
   353	                        "enable_auto_paste() in the harness rebinds it)",
   354	                    bound == true,
   355	                )
   356	                context.startActivity(
   357	                    Intent(context, com.envi.wispr.ui.VoiceInputActivity::class.java)
   358	                        .addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK),
   359	                )
   360	                assertTrue("The recorder never reported LISTENING; phase was ${phaseNow()}", listening.await(20, TimeUnit.SECONDS))
   361	                stage()
   362	                audio.deliver()
   363	                stopRequested.set(true)
   364	                context.startActivity(
   365	                    Intent(context, com.envi.wispr.ui.VoiceInputActivity::class.java)
   366	                        .putExtra(com.envi.wispr.ui.VoiceInputActivity.EXTRA_STOP, true)
   367	                        .addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK or Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_SINGLE_TOP),
   368	                )
   369	            } finally {
   370	                audio.close()
   371	                // The listener stays registered until awaitIdle/awaitFinalRow release it.
   372	            }
   373	        }
   374	
   375	        fun awaitIdle(): Boolean = try {
   376	            idle.await(90, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
   377	        } finally {
   378	            surfaceState.unregisterOnSharedPreferenceChangeListener(phaseListener)
   379	        }
   380	
   381	        /** The row the owner created for THIS take and finalised; the failure names the rows seen. */
   382	        fun awaitFinalRow(): TranscriptEntity {
   383	            val dao = EnviousWisprDatabase.get(context).transcriptDao()
   384	            var seen: List<TranscriptEntity> = emptyList()
   385	            val row = try {
   386	                runBlocking {
   387	                    withTimeoutOrNull(90_000) {
   388	                        dao.observeAll().first { rows ->
   389	                            seen = rows.filter { it.createdAtMs >= startedAtMs }
   390	                            seen.any { it.status in FINAL_STATUSES }
   391	                        }
   392	                    }
   393	                }
   394	            } finally {
   395	                surfaceState.unregisterOnSharedPreferenceChangeListener(phaseListener)
   396	            }
   397	            assertTrue(
   398	                "No History row for this take reached a final status within 90 s; rows since the start: " +
   399	                    seen.joinToString { "id=${it.id} status=${it.status} insertion=${it.insertionResult}" },
   400	                row != null,
   401	            )
   402	            return seen.first { it.status in FINAL_STATUSES }
   403	        }
   404	    }
   405	
   406	    /** Where the audio comes from: the driver outside the process, or the phone's speaker. */
   407	    private abstract inner class AudioSource {
   408	        abstract fun prepare()
   409	        abstract fun deliver()
   410	        open fun close() = Unit
   411	    }
   412	
   413	    private fun audioForThisRun(): AudioSource = when (val mode = arguments.getString(ARG_AUDIO, AUDIO_EXTERNAL)) {
   414	        AUDIO_EXTERNAL -> ExternalAudio()
   415	        AUDIO_SPEAKER -> SpeakerAudio()
   416	        else -> throw AssertionError("-e audio must be '$AUDIO_EXTERNAL' or '$AUDIO_SPEAKER', not '$mode'")
   417	    }
   418	
   419	    /**
   420	     * The driver feeds the audio. The done-receiver is registered BEFORE READY is published, so the
   421	     * driver's broadcast cannot arrive into nothing; the token keeps a stale broadcast from an earlier
   422	     * run out.
   423	     */
   424	    private inner class ExternalAudio : AudioSource() {
   425	        private val token = arguments.getString(ARG_DRIVER_TOKEN).orEmpty()
   426	        private val done = CountDownLatch(1)
   427	        private val receiver = object : BroadcastReceiver() {
   428	            override fun onReceive(c: Context, intent: Intent) {
   429	                if (intent.getStringExtra(ARG_DRIVER_TOKEN) == token) done.countDown()
   430	            }
   431	        }
   432	
   433	        override fun prepare() {
   434	            assertTrue("-e driver_token is required with -e audio external", token.matches(Regex("[0-9a-f]{8,64}")))
   435	            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 33) {
   436	                context.registerReceiver(receiver, IntentFilter(ACTION_EXTERNAL_AUDIO_DONE), Context.RECEIVER_EXPORTED)
   437	            } else {
   438	                @Suppress("UnspecifiedRegisterReceiverFlag")
   439	                context.registerReceiver(receiver, IntentFilter(ACTION_EXTERNAL_AUDIO_DONE))
   440	            }
   441	        }
   442	
   443	        override fun deliver() {
   444	            InstrumentationRegistry.getInstrumentation().sendStatus(
   445	                DRIVER_READY_STATUS,
   446	                Bundle().apply { putString(DRIVER_PHASE_KEY, "READY"); putString(ARG_DRIVER_TOKEN, token) },
   447	            )
   448	            assertTrue("the driver never reported the audio done (staging, not the product)", done.await(60, TimeUnit.SECONDS))
   449	        }
   450	
   451	        override fun close() {
   452	            runCatching { context.unregisterReceiver(receiver) }
   453	        }
   454	    }
   455	
   456	    /** The phone's own speaker plays the cached fixture; completion is the track's marker, not the clock. */
   457	    private inner class SpeakerAudio : AudioSource() {
   458	        override fun prepare() {
   459	            assumeTrue("Physical-phone fixture is missing", File(fixturePath).isFile)
   460	            assertTrue("-e expected_final is required with -e audio speaker", arguments.containsKey(ARG_EXPECTED_FINAL))
   461	        }
   462	
   463	        override fun deliver() {
   464	            val pcm = File(fixturePath).readBytes()
   465	            val audioManager = context.getSystemService(AudioManager::class.java)
   466	            val originalVolume = audioManager.getStreamVolume(AudioManager.STREAM_MUSIC)
   467	            val testVolume = minOf(5, audioManager.getStreamMaxVolume(AudioManager.STREAM_MUSIC))
   468	            val audioTrack = AudioTrack.Builder()
   469	                .setAudioAttributes(
   470	                    AudioAttributes.Builder()
   471	                        .setUsage(AudioAttributes.USAGE_MEDIA)
   472	                        .setContentType(AudioAttributes.CONTENT_TYPE_SPEECH)
   473	                        .build()
   474	                )
   475	                .setAudioFormat(
   476	                    AudioFormat.Builder()
   477	                        .setEncoding(AudioFormat.ENCODING_PCM_16BIT)
   478	                        .setSampleRate(16_000)
   479	                        .setChannelMask(AudioFormat.CHANNEL_OUT_MONO)
   480	                        .build()
   481	                )
   482	                .setBufferSizeInBytes(pcm.size)
   483	                .setTransferMode(AudioTrack.MODE_STATIC)
   484	                .build()
   485	            val played = CountDownLatch(1)
   486	            try {
   487	                audioManager.setStreamVolume(AudioManager.STREAM_MUSIC, testVolume, 0)
   488	                assertEquals("Phone playback was not queued in full", pcm.size, audioTrack.write(pcm, 0, pcm.size))
   489	                audioTrack.setPlaybackPositionUpdateListener(object : AudioTrack.OnPlaybackPositionUpdateListener {
   490	                    override fun onMarkerReached(track: AudioTrack) = played.countDown()
   491	                    override fun onPeriodicNotification(track: AudioTrack) = Unit
   492	                })
   493	                audioTrack.notificationMarkerPosition = pcm.size / 2
   494	                audioTrack.play()
   495	                assertTrue(
   496	                    "the speaker never reached the clip's last frame (staging, not the product)",
   497	                    played.await(pcm.size / 32L + 5_000, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS),
   498	                )
   499	            } finally {
   500	                audioTrack.stop()
   501	                audioTrack.release()
   502	                audioManager.setStreamVolume(AudioManager.STREAM_MUSIC, originalVolume, 0)
   503	            }
   504	        }
   505	    }
   506	
   507	    // ---- the rig ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
   508	
   509	    private fun startRig(twoFields: Boolean) {
   510	        context.startActivity(
   511	            Intent()
   512	                .setClassName(TEST_PACKAGE, "com.envi.wispr.PasteTargetActivity")
   513	                .putExtra(PasteTargetActivity.EXTRA_TWO_FIELDS, twoFields)
   514	                .addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK or Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_CLEAR_TOP),
   515	        )
   516	        // THE RIG'S readiness, not the subject's: the rig lives in another UID and can signal this process
   517	        // only through a file it writes in onCreate (the empty receipt), read through the shell. This is a
   518	        // bounded existence check on harness scaffolding, named in the plan's wait table as such.
   519	        val deadline = System.currentTimeMillis() + 15_000
   520	        while (System.currentTimeMillis() < deadline) {
   521	            if (receiptExists(PasteTargetActivity.RECEIPT_NAME)) return
   522	            Thread.sleep(100)
   523	        }
   524	        throw AssertionError("the paste-target rig did not come up (no receipt file after 15 s)")
   525	    }
   526	
   527	    /** The rig answers 1 only once editor B holds focus; anything else is a staging failure. */
   528	    private fun moveFocusToB() {
   529	        val answered = CountDownLatch(1)
   530	        var code = -1
   531	        context.sendOrderedBroadcast(
   532	            Intent(PasteTargetActivity.ACTION_FOCUS_B).setPackage(TEST_PACKAGE),
   533	            null,
   534	            object : BroadcastReceiver() {
   535	                override fun onReceive(c: Context, intent: Intent) {
   536	                    code = resultCode
   537	                    answered.countDown()
   538	                }
   539	            },
   540	            null,
   541	            0,
   542	            null,
   543	            null,
   544	        )
   545	        assertTrue("the rig never answered the focus request (staging)", answered.await(10, TimeUnit.SECONDS))
   546	        assertEquals("editor B did not take focus (staging)", 1, code)
   547	    }
   548	
   549	    // `executeShellCommand` runs the command directly, not through a shell, so no quoting and no `&&`:
   550	    // `ls <path>` prints the path when it exists and an error otherwise.
   551	    private fun receiptExists(name: String): Boolean =
   552	        shell("run-as $TEST_PACKAGE ls files/$name").trim() == "files/$name"
   553	
   554	    private fun receipt(name: String): String {
   555	        assertTrue("the receipt $name does not exist; the rig never wrote it", receiptExists(name))
   556	        return shell("run-as $TEST_PACKAGE cat files/$name")
   557	    }
   558	
   559	    private fun expectedFinal(): String = arguments.getString(ARG_EXPECTED_FINAL) ?: EXPECTED_TEXT
   560	
   561	    private fun occurrences(text: String, needle: String): Int =
   562	        if (needle.isEmpty()) 0 else Regex(Regex.escape(needle)).findAll(text).count()
   563	
   564	    /** The handoff the session owner logged for the run that just finished, or `"none"`. */
   565	    private fun observedHandoff(): String {
   566	        val logs = shell("logcat -d -v brief -s DictationSession:I '*:S'")
   567	        return Regex("\\(handoff=([A-Z_]+)\\)").findAll(logs).lastOrNull()?.groupValues?.get(1)
   568	            ?: "none"
   569	    }
   570	
   571	    private fun <T> bind(service: Class<*>, wrap: (IBinder?) -> T): Pair<T, ServiceConnection> {
   572	        val connected = CountDownLatch(1)
   573	        var bound: T? = null
   574	        val connection = object : ServiceConnection {
   575	            override fun onServiceConnected(name: ComponentName?, binder: IBinder?) {
   576	                bound = wrap(binder)
   577	                connected.countDown()
   578	            }
   579	
   580	            override fun onServiceDisconnected(name: ComponentName?) = Unit
   581	        }
   582	        context.bindService(Intent(context, service), connection, Context.BIND_AUTO_CREATE)
   583	        assertTrue("${service.simpleName} did not connect", connected.await(15, TimeUnit.SECONDS))
   584	        return bound!! to connection
   585	    }
   586	
   587	    /**
   588	     * Shell reads through UiAutomation WITHOUT suppressing accessibility services. A plain `uiAutomation`
   589	     * connection makes the system unbind every other accessibility service for as long as it is held
   590	     * (the trap that removed Appium in #177), so a test using it kills the paste service it means to
   591	     * observe and every take falls back to the clipboard (seen live 2026-09-22: handoff=SERVICE_NOT_RUNNING).
   592	     */
   593	    private fun shell(command: String): String {
   594	        val descriptor = InstrumentationRegistry.getInstrumentation()
   595	            .getUiAutomation(UiAutomation.FLAG_DONT_SUPPRESS_ACCESSIBILITY_SERVICES)
   596	            .executeShellCommand(command)
   597	        return FileInputStream(descriptor.fileDescriptor).bufferedReader().use { it.readText() }
   598	            .also { descriptor.close() }
   599	    }
   600	}

    23	/**
    24	 * Temporary installed-test target used to prove accessibility paste without touching user data.
    25	 *
    26	 * JAVA ON PURPOSE, like the other two rigs. This activity runs in the TEST package's own process, which

    29	 * {@code runCatching}: both are stdlib, the write threw {@code ClassNotFoundException}, the catch swallowed
    30	 * it, and the receipt file was never written (found live 2026-09-22, #161). Nothing here may reach a
    31	 * Kotlin class, and {@code android.util.Log} is called directly because the app's {@code DebugLogger} is
    32	 * an app class this process does not have; the two lines it can write carry a class name, never content.
    33	 *
    34	 * One editor (A) by default; with {@link #EXTRA_TWO_FIELDS} a second editor (B) below it, for the
    35	 * two-editor side-button case (#161 T3, REF-01): a take started with A focused must land in A even after
    36	 * focus moved to B. Each editor writes its WHOLE text to its own receipt file on every change, and both
    37	 * receipts are written EMPTY at creation, before the watchers are installed, so "untouched" is an existing
    38	 * empty file and "the editor never received anything" is not the same as "the file is missing".
    39	 */

    41	
    42	    /**
    43	     * The live field, so a test in THIS process could read what the editor received. The instrumented
    44	     * test runs in the app's process and cannot; it reads the receipt files instead.

    46	    private static volatile EditText field;
    47	
    48	    /** Read from outside the process with {@code run-as com.envi.wispr.test cat files/<this>}. */
    49	    public static final String RECEIPT_NAME = "paste-target-received.txt";
    50	
    51	    /** Editor B's receipt, present only when {@link #EXTRA_TWO_FIELDS} was set. */
    52	    public static final String RECEIPT_B_NAME = "paste-target-b-received.txt";
    53	
    54	    public static final String EXTRA_TWO_FIELDS = "two_fields";
    55	
    56	    /**
    57	     * An ORDERED broadcast that moves focus to editor B and answers with result code 1 only once
    58	     * {@code B.requestFocus()} returned true and {@code B.hasFocus()} reads true; 0 otherwise. Sent by
    59	     * the instrumentation from the APP's process, so the runtime receiver is exported (this activity runs
    60	     * under the test package's UID) and the intent is explicit to {@code com.envi.wispr.test}.
    61	     */
    62	    public static final String ACTION_FOCUS_B = "com.envi.wispr.test.FOCUS_B";
    63	
    64	    public static EditText getField() {
    65	        return field;
    66	    }
    67	
    68	    public static String currentText() {
    69	        EditText live = field;
    70	        return live == null ? "" : live.getText().toString();
    71	    }
    72	
    73	    private EditText fieldB;
    74	    private BroadcastReceiver focusReceiver;
    75	
    76	    @Override
    77	    protected void onCreate(Bundle savedInstanceState) {
    78	        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState);
    79	        Intent intent = getIntent();
    80	        boolean twoFields = intent != null && intent.getBooleanExtra(EXTRA_TWO_FIELDS, false);
    81	        final EditText a = editor("Silent auto-paste target");
    82	        final EditText b = twoFields ? editor("Second editor, never the target") : null;
    83	        LinearLayout column = new LinearLayout(this);
    84	        column.setOrientation(LinearLayout.VERTICAL);
    85	        column.addView(a, new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(LinearLayout.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, 0, 1f));
    86	        if (b != null) {
    87	            column.addView(b, new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(LinearLayout.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, 0, 1f));
    88	        }
    89	        setContentView(column);
    90	        field = a;
    91	        fieldB = b;
    92	
    93	        // The field writes what it receives to a file, because instrumentation runs in the APP's process
    94	        // while this activity runs in the TEST package's, so a static here is not readable from a test.
    95	        // The file is the oracle: it is the editor's own content, written by the editor. BOTH receipts
    96	        // exist and are EMPTY before either watcher is installed.
    97	        final File receiptA = new File(getFilesDir(), RECEIPT_NAME);
    98	        final File receiptB = new File(getFilesDir(), RECEIPT_B_NAME);
    99	        write(receiptA, "");
   100	        if (b != null) {
   101	            write(receiptB, "");
   102	        } else if (receiptB.exists() && !receiptB.delete()) {
   103	            android.util.Log.w("PasteTarget", "stale B receipt could not be deleted");
   104	        }
   105	        a.addTextChangedListener(watcher(receiptA));
   106	        if (b != null) {
   107	            b.addTextChangedListener(watcher(receiptB));
   108	            BroadcastReceiver receiver = new BroadcastReceiver() {
   109	                @Override
   110	                public void onReceive(Context context, Intent received) {
   111	                    boolean moved = b.requestFocus() && b.hasFocus();
   112	                    setResultCode(moved ? 1 : 0);
   113	                }
   114	            };
   115	            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 33) {
   116	                registerReceiver(receiver, new IntentFilter(ACTION_FOCUS_B), Context.RECEIVER_EXPORTED);
   117	            } else {
   118	                registerReceiver(receiver, new IntentFilter(ACTION_FOCUS_B));
   119	            }
   120	            focusReceiver = receiver;
   121	        }
   122	
   123	        a.requestFocus();

   125	                .showSoftInput(a, InputMethodManager.SHOW_IMPLICIT), 250);
   126	    }
   127	
   128	    private EditText editor(String hintText) {
   129	        EditText view = new EditText(this);
   130	        view.setHint(hintText);
   131	        view.setTextSize(18f);
   132	        view.setSingleLine(false);
   133	        view.setGravity(Gravity.TOP);
   134	        view.setPadding(48, 48, 48, 48);
   135	        return view;
   136	    }
   137	
   138	    private static TextWatcher watcher(final File receipt) {
   139	        return new TextWatcher() {
   140	            @Override public void beforeTextChanged(CharSequence s, int a, int b, int c) { }
   141	            @Override public void onTextChanged(CharSequence s, int a, int b, int c) { }
   142	            @Override public void afterTextChanged(Editable s) {
   143	                write(receipt, s == null ? "" : s.toString());
   144	            }
   145	        };
   146	    }
   147	
   148	    /** Plain java.io, whole file, so a receipt is the editor's text and nothing else. */
   149	    private static void write(File receipt, String text) {
   150	        try (OutputStream out = new FileOutputStream(receipt, false)) {
   151	            out.write(text.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
   152	        } catch (IOException error) {
   153	            android.util.Log.w("PasteTarget", "receipt write failed: " + error.getClass().getSimpleName());
   154	        }
   155	    }
   156	
   157	    @Override
   158	    protected void onDestroy() {
   159	        if (focusReceiver != null) {
   160	            try {
   161	                unregisterReceiver(focusReceiver);
   162	            } catch (IllegalArgumentException ignored) {
   163	                // already unregistered
   164	            }
   165	            focusReceiver = null;
   166	        }
   167	        // A reference to a destroyed activity's field would let a test read the PREVIOUS run's text and
   168	        // call it this run's result, which is the plausible-value trap wearing a green tick.
   169	        EditText live = field;
   170	        if (live != null && live.getContext() == this) {
   171	            field = null;
   172	        }
   173	        fieldB = null;
   174	        super.onDestroy();
   175	    }
   176	}

     1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
     2	# One end-to-end dictation on the phone: launcher toggle, the speech fixture through the speaker, stop,
     3	# then wait for the handoff line. Writes the run's logcat to <outdir>/<label>.log and prints the timeline.
     4	#
     5	#   scripts/uat/dictate-once.sh <label> <outdir> [device] [record-seconds]   (default 12.5, the short fixture plus margin)
     6	#
     7	# Preconditions the caller checks (residency-campaign.sh does): Bluetooth off (a headset steals the speaker
     8	# and the microphone hears nothing), screen awake and unlocked, both APKs from this build installed, the
     9	# fixture present and non-empty in both packages' cache (phone-audio-playback.md).
    10	set -uo pipefail
    11	LABEL="$1"; OUT="$2"; D="${3:-100.94.206.47:5555}"; RECORD_S="${4:-12.5}"; mkdir -p "$OUT"
    12	adb -s "$D" logcat -c
    13	adb -s "$D" shell "am start -n com.envi.wispr/.ui.VoiceInputActivity --ez toggle true" >/dev/null
    14	sleep 1.5
    15	adb -s "$D" shell "am start -n com.envi.wispr.test/com.envi.wispr.SpeakerPlaybackActivity" >/dev/null
    16	sleep "$RECORD_S"
    17	adb -s "$D" shell "am start -n com.envi.wispr/.ui.VoiceInputActivity --ez stop true" >/dev/null
    18	LOG=""
    19	for _ in $(seq 1 80); do
    20	  LOG=$(adb -s "$D" logcat -d -v time -s 'DictationSession:*' 'PolishService:*' 'AsrService:*' 'PasteAccessibility:*' 'ActivityManager:*' 'lmkd:*' 2>/dev/null)
    21	  if echo "$LOG" | grep -q "Auto-insert handed\|kept on clipboard\|showError\|Transcription failed\|Speech recognition failed\|No audio captured"; then break; fi
    22	  sleep 0.5
    23	done
    24	printf '%s\n' "$LOG" > "$OUT/$LABEL.log"
    25	printf '%s\n' "$LOG" | grep -i "recording_stop\|asr_request\|result_received\|S1-mini loaded\|polish_done\|Polish outcome\|Polish result received\|Auto-insert handed\|kept on clipboard\|showError\|failed\|Killing\|lowmemory\|lmk" \
    26	  | sed 's/^\(..-.. ..:..:..\.[0-9]*\).*: \(.*\)$/\1 \2/'

docs/feature-requests/issue-161-2026-09-22-harness-true-verdicts.md:17:The harness stops passing on weak evidence: a take's verdict requires the current take's own `insertion api=` line with `outcome=VERIFIED`, `written=true` and the right target, reports the route (COMMIT or PASTE) as its own line, compares the editor's WHOLE final text with an explicit expected value, accepts only a uniquely FOCUSED editor as the target, reads `bound()` from the whole bound-services block, bounds its log reads to the take it owns, keys its restore book by the phone's hardware identity, says `NOT RUN` (never `BLOCKED`) when recording is off, and can wrap a take in an owned, journaled screen recording. The dormant speaker-playback path (`stage_phone_speech` (removed) and friends, the windowed `SpeakerPlaybackActivity` launch) is deleted from the harness, superseded by #162's silent injector on the phone and gRPC on the emulator; the proposed test-APK playback Service is not built. `VoicePipelineDeviceTest` stops sleeping and polling logcat: its two take rows wait on the session owner's phase writes and the History row's insertion outcome, assert the destination editor's whole text from the editor's own receipt file, exactly once, the non-target editor unchanged, and the stored route; the audio is fed by the driver (harness) when the test reports READY on the instrumentation stream, on the emulator over gRPC and on the phone through the #162 injector, so the test itself never plays a speaker. A two-editor side-button row (REF-01) joins it.

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	# Phone Audio Playback
     2	
     3	**The default real take on the phone is NOT this page.** Since #162 it is the silent injector,
     4	`device-testing.md` FACT: silent-physical-phone-audio-injection-for-night-uat (`scripts/uat/silent-audio/run.py`),
     5	and since #161 the harness (`wispr_eyes.py`) has no speaker-playback path at all. This page keeps the
     6	SPEAKER route for what still uses it: the three instrumentation fixtures that start
     7	`SpeakerPlaybackActivity` (`SilenceStoppedTakeTranscribesDeviceTest`, `CaptureWithSilenceStopDeviceTest`,
     8	`SilenceStopEndToEndDeviceTest`) and `scripts/uat/dictate-once.sh` (the residency campaign's driver).
     9	The fixture is rendered OFF the phone (Azure Speech to PCM, or `say` plus `ffmpeg`) and pushed; the
    10	phone only plays it. It is not streamed from the MacBook and does not use the MacBook speakers.
    11	
    12	## FACT: the-test-helper-lives-only-in-the-test-apk
    13	
    14	`SpeakerPlaybackActivity` is declared only by the Android test APK. Installing or launching only the
    15	normal application cannot expose it. The helper uses Android's offline US text-to-speech voice, speaks at
    16	0.85 speed and full per-utterance volume, then closes itself.
    17	
    18	The helper reads its custom sentence from the test package's private
    19	`files/speaker-utterance.txt`. It does not read an intent extra. The file is deleted after it is read. If
    20	the file is missing, empty, or larger than 500 bytes, the helper says:
    21	
    22	> This is a physical phone insertion test.
    23	
    24	## PROCEDURE: play-a-custom-sentence-through-the-phone
    25	
    26	From the repository root:
    27	
    28	```bash
    29	SERIAL=100.94.206.47:5555
    30	PROJECT=/Users/m4pro_sv/Developer/EnviousLabs/EnviousWispr-Android
    31	
    32	cd "$PROJECT"
    33	./gradlew :app:assembleDebugAndroidTest --no-daemon --console=plain
    34	
    35	adb -s "$SERIAL" install -r "$PROJECT/app/build/outputs/apk/debug/app-debug.apk"
    36	adb -s "$SERIAL" install -r -t "$PROJECT/app/build/outputs/apk/androidTest/debug/app-debug-androidTest.apk"
    37	
    38	adb -s "$SERIAL" shell run-as com.envi.wispr.test mkdir -p files
    39	printf '%s' 'This sentence is playing through my phone speaker.' \
    40	  | adb -s "$SERIAL" shell \
    41	      'run-as com.envi.wispr.test sh -c "cat > files/speaker-utterance.txt"'
    42	
    43	adb -s "$SERIAL" shell am start \
    44	  -n com.envi.wispr.test/com.envi.wispr.SpeakerPlaybackActivity
    45	```
    46	
    47	Use `-t` when installing the Android test APK because Android marks it as test-only. If ADB cannot reach
    48	the fixed Tailscale address, recover the connection first using the procedure in
    49	[`device-testing.md`](device-testing.md).
    50	
    51	## PROCEDURE: run-the-full-phone-speaker-pipeline-test
    52	
    53	The full instrumented user-path test uses a different source. It reads a 16 kHz, mono, signed PCM fixture
    54	from the test package cache and plays it through Android's media output while EnviousWispr records from
    55	the physical microphone. The test temporarily raises media volume to at most level 5 and restores the
    56	original volume afterward.
    57	
    58	The required private fixture path is:
    59	
    60	```text
    61	cache/enviouswispr-uat.pcm
    62	```
    63	
    64	The test method is:
    65	
    66	```text
    67	com.envi.wispr.VoicePipelineDeviceTest#launcherRecordsPhonePlaybackAndReachesClipboardStep
    68	```
    69	
    70	This is valid physical-device pipeline evidence only when the test actually runs. A missing fixture makes
    71	the instrumented test skip; report that as not run, never passed.
    72	
    73	## RULE: diagnose-the-right-audio-path
    74	
    75	- If no sound plays, first confirm that both APKs are installed and that phone media volume is audible.
    76	- Do not pass the sentence as an activity intent extra; the helper ignores it.
    77	- Do not use MacBook audio as evidence when the phone and laptop are in different locations.
    78	- Do not confuse offline text-to-speech playback with the PCM fixture used by the full pipeline test.
    79	- **`termux-tts-speak` and `termux-media-player` BLOCK FOREVER** when Termux:API is not answering, and a
    80	  blocked SSH call reads as a slow one. Neither is a shortcut past the two procedures above.
    81	- Restore any phone volume or other device setting changed outside the automated test.
    82	
    15	`scripts/uat/wispr_eyes.py` sees and drives the running app on the founder's phone. One Python call per
    16	errand; never a coordinate you worked out yourself.
    17	
    18	```bash
    19	python3 -c "import sys; sys.path.insert(0, 'scripts/uat'); from wispr_eyes import *; <calls>"
    20	```
    21	
    22	**Recording is OFF.** `open_recorder` refuses and `check_recorder`, `room_is_quiet` and `dictate_emulator`
    23	answer `NOT RUN: recording from this harness is off …` (`RECORDING_IS_OFF` in the script says why). Report
    24	any voice question as that `NOT RUN` line. Never work around it. The speaker-playback calls
    25	(`stage_phone_speech`, `say_on_phone`, `test_dictation`) are gone (#161); the phone's real take is the
    26	silent helper below.
    27	
    28	**To run a REAL take on the physical phone, use the silent helper, not these calls.**
    29	`scripts/uat/silent-audio/run.py` drives a full take (start, inject test audio silently, stop, verify the
    30	editor's own text) with no speaker sound, and is fail-closed with its own restore. It is the default for
    31	night UAT. Contract, one-call form, the `logcat -c` gotcha and evidence:
    32	[`../../knowledge/device-testing.md`](../../knowledge/device-testing.md) FACT:
    33	silent-physical-phone-audio-injection-for-night-uat.
    34	
    35	## Order of every run
    36	
    37	1. `print(device(), ready(), bound())`. `Blocked: the phone is locked` or `Blocked: no physical phone
    38	   attached` means report BLOCKED and stop; never send input at a lock screen. That is the PHYSICAL
    39	   phone: its PIN is Saurabh's. The emulator is ours; see "The emulator" below. `bound()` False means
    40	   `enable_auto_paste()`.
    41	2. Ask the question with the fewest calls (table below). At four calls, stop: a higher-level call exists.
    42	3. `print(restore())`. Always, even after a failure. It reads `~/.cache/wispr-eyes/restore.json`, so it
    43	   works from any later session.
    44	
    45	## The calls
    46	
    47	| Question | Call |
    48	|---|---|
    49	| What is on screen | `look()` |
    50	| Where is one control | `find('Remove')`; refuses when two match, so narrow it or open the page first |
    51	| Press it | `tap('Storage')` |
    52	| Change tab / open a menu page | `open_tab('Transcription')` / `nav('Storage')` |
    53	| See more / bring a control into view | `scroll('down')` / `reveal('Stop recording on silence')` |
    54	| Read one switch / every switch here | `switch('Smart insertion')` / `switches()` |
    55	| Set a switch (the page name is required so it can be put back) | `set_switch('Smart insertion', True, where='Clipboard')` |
    56	| Walk every screen, every switch | `scan()`; `scan(toggle=True)` also flips and restores each one, about two minutes |
    57	| Is the floating recorder up | `overlay()`; the ONLY eye that sees it |
    58	| Is a take running / what did the last one do | `recording()` / `last_take()` (bounded to the window `clear_log()` opened; `insertion` is the parsed outcome line; `unknown` names two takes in one window) |
    59	| Force-stop the app (turns auto-paste back on) | `stop_app()` |
    60	
    61	Shell form: `python3 scripts/uat/wispr_eyes.py look|find|tap|tab|nav|scan|switches|logs|take|shot|restore`.
    62	
    63	## Which eye sees what
    64	
    65	The accessibility tree (`look`, `find`) and screenshots (`shot`) see the app's pages and NOT the floating
    66	recorder. Only `overlay()` sees the recorder. Two eyes agreeing that something is absent is the tell to
    67	reach for a third before writing a defect. Appium is gone from this project (#177): a UiAutomator2
    68	session unbinds the accessibility service that draws the recorder. When `uiautomator dump` dies twice
    69	with 137, `tree()` answers `Blocked` naming `shot()`, `overlay()` and `last_take()`; use those.
    70	
    71	## The eye is fast on a debug build
    72	
    73	`tree()`, and everything on it (`look`, `find`, `tap`, `switch`), reads the screen from the app's own
    74	accessibility service in 0.1 s on a DEBUG build with the service bound (#181); on a release build or an
    75	unbound service it falls back to `uiautomator dump` at 1.9 s a read. The fast eye sees every window,
    76	including the recorder pill during a take, which the slow eye never did; `overlay()` stays the
    77	authority for the pill until it is moved onto the fast eye. `_STATE["eye"]` says which eye answered.
    78	
    79	## The emulator
    80	
    81	`WISPR_SERIAL=emulator-5554` in the environment points every call at the AVD instead of refusing for
    82	"no physical phone". The harness is the ONLY door onto it (#177, 2026-09-20): the Bash hook refuses raw
    83	`adb shell input|uiautomator|screencap`, `adb emu` and `grpcurl` at the emulator controller, and names
    84	the call to use. The emulator is the one device this harness unlocks (its PIN is ours) and records on
    85	(a leaked take there costs nothing; `restore()` ends it). The phone is never unlocked and never recorded
    86	on from here.
    87	
    88	| Question | Call |
    89	|---|---|
    90	| Boot it with the control service on | `launch_emulator()`; `restart=True` to kill and reboot |
    91	| Open its lock screen | `ready()` does it; `unlock_emulator()` alone, one PIN attempt, read back |
    92	| Host microphone on or off | `set_host_mic(False)`; journaled, `restore()` puts it back from any process |
    93	| Feed a PCM file into the microphone | `inject_audio('utt.pcm')`, only inside `with open_recorder():` |
    94	| One spoken take, judged by the editor's WHOLE text against your literal | `dictate_emulator('and I will send the deck tomorrow', expected_final='And I will send the deck tomorrow. ')`; `route='COMMIT'` to require the route; `record='/tmp/take.mp4'` for an owned screen recording |
    95	| Insert text with no audio (DEBUG build) | `debug_insert('The quarterly report is ready.', expected_final='The quarterly report is ready. ')` |
    96	| Run one on-device test row with the harness feeding the audio | `run_device_test('com.envi.wispr.VoicePipelineDeviceTest#aSideButtonTakeLandsInTheFocusedEditorExactlyOnce', 'the quarterly marker sentence lands tomorrow')` |
    97	| Freeze (a debugger suspend; `kill -STOP` does not take there), thaw or kill one of our processes by verified pid (#115 wedge scenes) | `freeze_process('com.envi.wispr:audio')` / `thaw_process(...)` / `kill_process(...)`; a freeze is journaled and `restore()` thaws it from any later process |
    98	| Bring an app up / open a page / focus an editor | `open_app('com.google.android.gm')` / `open_page('about:blank')` / `focus_field('Compose email', 'com.google.android.gm')` |
    99	
   100	Shell form: `WISPR_SERIAL=emulator-5554 python3 scripts/uat/wispr_eyes.py launch|unlock|mic on|off|inject <pcm>|dictate-emu [sentence]|insert <text>`.
   101	A routine success prints ONE line (`OK <command>: …`); a finding prints every line, and `--verbose` prints
   102	the notes on success too. Read the one line; ask for `--verbose` only when it is red.
   103	
   104	A take on the emulator (`dictate_emulator`) needs a focused editor first: `open_app`, `tap('Compose',
   105	package='com.google.android.gm')`, `focus_field('Compose email', 'com.google.android.gm')`. Proven
   106	2026-09-20: the sentence landed by the COMMIT route and the editor's own text matched.
   107	
   108	`debug_insert` bypasses audio, so it cannot reproduce an audio-timing race; for speech itself use
   109	`dictate_emulator`. Judge both by the editor's own text, never the clipboard, which is the app's fallback.
   110	`scripts/uat/field-sweep.sh` reports focus, keyboard and bubble presence; `scripts/uat/rapid-fire.sh`
   111	runs takes back to back.
   112	
   113	## Never press
   114	
   115	`Remove` on a model card, `Delete all` on History, Dictionary `Add` / `Import` / `Export`. They delete
   116	his models, his history or his word list. Never clear app data on his phone to reach first-run setup;
   117	`Permissions` then `Continue guided setup` resumes it in place. Never run `connectedDebugAndroidTest`
   118	against this phone: it uninstalls the app and its models.
   119	
   120	## Report

No polish engine assertion (the emulator has none; the polish limb's outcome is `transcribesThenPolishesWithSavedCustomWords`'s business).
docs/feature-requests/issue-161-2026-09-22-harness-true-verdicts.md:85:T3. **The two-editor side-button case (REF-01).** `PasteTargetActivity` gains an intent extra `two_fields` (proposed): a second `EditText` B below A with its own receipt `paste-target-b-received.txt`. On creation the activity atomically writes `""` to BOTH receipt files BEFORE installing the text watchers (today it deletes the file and creates it only on the first change, `PasteTargetActivity.kt:55-62`; coverage F5), so an untouched B is an EXISTING empty receipt and a disabled editor write leaves A's receipt empty, which fails.  succeeded in 0ms:
        def send_signal(self, sig):
            """Send a signal to the process."""
            # bpo-38630: Polling reduces the risk of sending a signal to the
            # wrong process if the process completed, the Popen.returncode
            # attribute is still None, and the pid has been reassigned
            # (recycled) to a new different process. This race condition can
            # happens in two cases.
            #
            # Case 1. Thread A calls Popen.poll(), thread B calls
            # Popen.send_signal(). In thread A, waitpid() succeed and returns
            # the exit status. Thread B calls kill() because poll() in thread A
            # did not set returncode yet. Calling poll() in thread B prevents
            # the race condition thanks to Popen._waitpid_lock.
            #
            # Case 2. waitpid(pid, 0) has been called directly, without
            # using Popen methods: returncode is still None is this case.
            # Calling Popen.poll() will set returncode to a default value,
            # since waitpid() fails with ProcessLookupError.
            self.poll()
            if self.returncode is not None:
                # Skip signalling a process that we know has already died.
                return

            # The race condition can still happen if the race condition
            # described above happens between the returncode test
            # and the kill() call.
            try:
                os.kill(self.pid, sig)
            except ProcessLookupError:
                # Suppress the race condition error; bpo-40550.
                pass

  2468	              and (target_package is None or n["package"] == target_package)
  2469	              and n["focused"]]
  2470	    if not fields:
  2471	        return None
  2472	    if len(fields) > 1:
  2473	        raise Blocked(f"{len(fields)} focused editors are on screen, so which one the words were "
  2474	                      "meant for is a guess")

  2476	    return (node["package"], node["id"] or node["bounds"], node["text"])

  2479	def _field_by_identity(package, identity):
  2480	    """The ONE editor with this identity in the package, as (package, identity, text); None when gone.
  2481	
  2482	    The AFTER read of a take (#161, grounded round 1): focus is not required here, because the harness's
  2483	    own launcher pauses the editor and hides its keyboard for a moment, and a verdict that needed focus
  2484	    back would fail for the harness's reason, not the app's. Identity is the resource id, or the bounds
  2485	    when the editor has none. Two matches is a refusal, never a pick.
  2486	    """
  2487	    fields = [n for n in tree(refresh=True) if n["kind"] == "EditText" and n["package"] == package
  2488	              and (n["id"] or n["bounds"]) == identity]
  2489	    if not fields:
  2490	        return None
  2491	    if len(fields) > 1:
  2492	        raise Blocked(f"{len(fields)} editors in {package} share the identity {identity!r}, so which one "
  2493	                      "holds the words is a guess")
  2494	    node = fields[0]
  2495	    return (node["package"], node["id"] or node["bounds"], node["text"])

  2498	def _plain(text):
  2499	    """A text with only the app's own decoration removed: NFKC, case-folded, punctuation dropped,
  2500	    whitespace collapsed. NOT a tokenizer: three review rounds each found a script a tokenizer broke
  2501	    (Cyrillic, combining marks, unspaced CJK), so the verdict is a plain substring match on this."""
  2502	    import unicodedata
  2503	    folded = unicodedata.normalize("NFKC", text or "").casefold()
  2504	    kept = "".join(c for c in folded if not unicodedata.category(c).startswith("P"))
  2505	    return " ".join(kept.split())

  2508	def _excerpt(text, keep=80):
  2509	    """The tail of an editor's text, so a report line stays one line whatever the draft has grown to."""
  2510	    text = (text or "").replace("\xa0", " ").strip()
  2511	    return repr(text if len(text) <= keep else "…" + text[-keep:]) + f" ({len(text)} chars)"

  2514	def open_app(package):
  2515	    """Bring an app to the front by its package, the way the launcher would, and assert it arrived."""
  2516	    ready()
  2517	    _adb(f"monkey -p {shlex.quote(package)} -c android.intent.category.LAUNCHER 1")
  2518	    for _ in range(8):
  2519	        time.sleep(0.5)
  2520	        _STATE["tree"] = None
  2521	        if package in {n["package"] for n in tree()}:
  2522	            return f"{package} is on screen"
  2523	    raise Blocked(f"{package} did not come to the front")

  2526	def focus_field(label, package):
  2527	    """Press an editor by its hint or text and READ BACK that it took focus.
  2528	
  2529	    An editor is pressable by definition, whatever the tree says about `clickable`, so the press lands
  2530	    on the label's own centre. The read-back is the point: a dictation aims at the focused field, and a
  2531	    press that did not focus it would send the words somewhere else.
  2532	    """
  2533	    tap(label, clickable=False, package=package)
  2534	    time.sleep(0.8)
  2535	    field = _focused_field(package)
  2536	    if field is None:
  2537	        raise Blocked(f"{label!r} was pressed but no editor in {package} has focus")
  2538	    return f"focused {label!r} ({field[1]})"

  2541	def dictate_emulator(sentence, target_package="com.google.android.gm", expected_final=None, route=None,
  2542	                     record=None):
  2543	    """One spoken take on the EMULATOR, fed over gRPC, judged by the editor's own whole text.
  2544	
  2545	    Replaces `scripts/uat/grpc-take.sh`, which tapped coordinates it had worked out by hand, turned the
  2546	    host microphone off and never back on, and trusted a log line. Here the take is owned by
  2547	    `open_recorder()`, the microphone by `set_host_mic()` and its journal, and the verdict by the editor
  2548	    that was uniquely focused before the take, read back by its identity after it.
  2549	
  2550	    `expected_final` is the caller's LITERAL whole final editor text (#161 finding 3): the speech engine
  2551	    adds its own punctuation and the insertion its capitalisation and spacing, so it cannot be derived
  2552	    from the sentence, and "the text changed" is not "the words landed". `route="COMMIT"` (or "PASTE")
  2553	    makes any other route an ISSUE; without it the route is reported, never assumed. `record` is a host
  2554	    path for an owned screen recording around the take (`screen_recording`).
  2555	    """
  2556	    _require_emulator("a spoken take")
  2557	    if _recording_is_off():
  2558	        return [f"NOT RUN: {RECORDING_IS_OFF}"]
  2559	    if not isinstance(expected_final, str):
  2560	        return ["BLOCKED: expected_final is required: the literal whole text the editor must hold after "
  2561	                "the take (the speech engine's punctuation and the insertion's spacing included)"]
  2562	    if route is not None and route not in ("COMMIT", "PASTE"):
  2563	        return ["BLOCKED: route must be 'COMMIT', 'PASTE' or None"]
  2564	    report = []
  2565	    restore()
  2566	    ready()
  2567	    if not bound():
  2568	        return ["BLOCKED: auto-paste is switched off, so nothing can be inserted. "
  2569	                "enable_auto_paste() turns it back on."]
  2570	    before = _focused_field(target_package)
  2571	    if before is None:
  2572	        return [f"BLOCKED: no focused editor in {target_package} is on screen; tap into one first"]
  2573	    scratch = os.path.join(os.environ.get("TMPDIR", "/tmp"), "wispr-eyes")
  2574	    os.makedirs(scratch, exist_ok=True)
  2575	    pcm = _pcm_from_sentence(sentence, os.path.join(scratch, f"utt-{uuid.uuid4().hex}.pcm"))
  2576	    report.append(f"NOTE: {sentence!r} was rendered to {pcm}")
  2577	    ending = None
  2578	    recording_note = None
  2579	    try:
  2580	        # OFF AS THE RESTING STATE, never a switch inside the take: see _rest_host_mic_off. Done before
  2581	        # the audio is rendered so the switch, if one happens at all, is as far from the capture as the
  2582	        # call allows.
  2583	        report.append(f"NOTE: {_rest_host_mic_off()}")
  2584	        clear_log()
  2585	        with screen_recording(record):
  2586	            with open_recorder(verify=False):
  2587	                # THE OWNER'S OWN LIVE LINE, not a warm-up sleep (#161, grounded round 2): the line is
  2588	                # written after the capture process admitted its first audio block, which is what
  2589	                # "the microphone is ready for the audio" means.
  2590	                report.append(f"NOTE: {_wait_for_owner_listening()}")
  2591	                report.append(f"NOTE: {inject_audio(pcm)}")
  2592	                time.sleep(2.0)
  2593	                stop_dictation()
  2594	                ending = _wait_for_the_take_to_finish()
  2595	    finally:
  2596	        for line in restore():
  2597	            report.append(f"NOTE: restored {line}")
  2598	    recording_note = _STATE.get("last_screen_recording") if record else None
  2599	    if recording_note:
  2600	        report.append(f"NOTE: {recording_note}")
  2601	    if ending is None:
  2602	        report.append("ISSUE: the dictation never reached an ending, so nothing can be said about it")
  2603	        return report
  2604	    take = last_take()
  2605	    if take["unknown"]:
  2606	        report.append(f"ISSUE: UNKNOWN, {take['unknown']}")
  2607	        return report
  2608	    report.append(f"VERIFIED: a take ran, ended by {take['ended_by']}" if take["ended_by"]
  2609	                  else "ISSUE: no take ending was logged")
  2610	    if take["transcribed_chars"]:
  2611	        report.append(f"VERIFIED: {take['transcribed_chars']} characters came back from the speech engine")
  2612	    else:
  2613	        report.append("ISSUE: nothing was transcribed; either the stream never reached the microphone "
  2614	                      "or the engine returned empty")
  2615	    report.extend(_judge_insertion(take, target_package, route))
  2616	    report.extend(_judge_editor(before, target_package, expected_final))

  2620	def _judge_insertion(take, target_package, route=None):
  2621	    """The take's own outcome line, field by field: VERIFIED only when it says so (#161 finding 2)."""
  2622	    outcome = take["insertion"]
  2623	    if take["insertions"] != 1 or outcome is None:
  2624	        return [f"ISSUE: {take['insertions']} insertion outcome lines were logged for this take, so its "
  2625	                "delivery cannot be read"]
  2626	    lines = [f"NOTE: insertion api={outcome['api']} route={outcome['route']} written={outcome['written']} "
  2627	             f"evidence={outcome['evidence']} outcome={outcome['outcome']} attempts={outcome['attempts']} "
  2628	             f"target={outcome['target']}"]
  2629	    problems = []
  2630	    if outcome["outcome"] != "VERIFIED":
  2631	        problems.append(f"outcome={outcome['outcome']}")
  2632	    if outcome["written"] != "true":
  2633	        problems.append(f"written={outcome['written']}")
  2634	    if target_package is not None and outcome["target"] != target_package:
  2635	        problems.append(f"target={outcome['target']} (wanted {target_package})")
  2636	    if route is not None and outcome["route"] != route:
  2637	        problems.append(f"route={outcome['route']} (wanted {route})")
  2638	    if problems:
  2639	        lines.append("ISSUE: the app's own outcome line does not say the words were delivered: "
  2640	                     + ", ".join(problems))
  2641	    else:
  2642	        lines.append(f"VERIFIED: route={outcome['route']}, the app's own outcome line says written, "
  2643	                     f"verified, into {outcome['target']}")
  2644	    return lines

  2647	def _judge_editor(before, target_package, expected_final):
  2648	    """The editor chosen BEFORE the take, read back by identity, its WHOLE text against the literal."""
  2649	    after = _field_by_identity(before[0], before[1])
  2650	    if after is None:
  2651	        return ["ISSUE: the editor that was focused before the take is no longer on screen, so where "
  2652	                "the words went is unknown"]
  2653	    focused_now = _focused_field(target_package)
  2654	    focus_note = ("the same editor is focused" if focused_now and focused_now[:2] == before[:2]
  2655	                  else "focus is elsewhere for now (not part of the verdict)")
  2656	    text = after[2] or ""
  2657	    if text == expected_final:
  2658	        return [f"VERIFIED: the editor's whole text now equals the expectation ({len(text)} chars); "
  2659	                f"{focus_note}"]
  2660	    # RAW tails, not `_excerpt`: that helper folds a no-break space into a space and strips, which hid the
  2661	    # only difference on the first live run (Gmail stores the inserted trailing space as U+00A0).
  2662	    return [f"ISSUE: the editor's whole text differs from the expectation; it ends {_raw_tail(text)}, "
  2663	            f"expected {_raw_tail(expected_final)}; {focus_note}"]
  2664

  3521	
  3522	# The lines that END a dictation, whatever the outcome. Polling for one of these is how
  3523	# `scripts/uat/dictate-once.sh` decides a run is over, and it beats a fixed sleep in both directions:
  3524	# a slow take is not cut off, and a fast one does not cost fourteen seconds of nothing.
  3525	# How long the recorder waits before the audio starts, and after it ends. Both are the smallest value
  3526	# that worked rather than a comfortable one: the lead-in covers the speaker activity launching, and the
  3527	# trailing margin covers the last word reaching the microphone. Everything beyond them is the founder
  3528	# watching his own recorder run on silence.
  3529	LEAD_IN_S = 1.5
  3530	TRAILING_MARGIN_S = 1.0
  3531	
  3532	_TERMINAL_LINES = (
  3533	    "Auto-insert handed", "kept on clipboard", "Transcription failed",
  3534	    "Speech recognition failed", "No audio captured", "showError",
  3535	    # `Take terminal: <STATE>` is the session's own ending line (seen `NO_SPEECH` on 2026-09-20); without
  3536	    # it a take that heard nothing waited the full 40 s and 200 log reads before saying so.
  3537	    "Take terminal:",
  3538	)

  3540	
  3541	def _stream_log_until(markers, seconds, tags):
  3542	    """ONE blocking `logcat` reader over this process's window; the first line carrying a marker wins.
  3543	
  3544	    Not a loop over `logcat -d` snapshots (#161, grounded rounds 3 and 4): a snapshot loop is a poll
  3545	    with a budget, and a budget that runs out reads exactly like the subject never answering. The
  3546	    timeout here is a failure bound, reported as None, never a product verdict.
  3547	    """
  3548	    filters = [f"{tag}:I" for tag in tags] + ["*:S"]
  3549	    since = _STATE["log_since"]
  3550	    args = [ADB, "-s", device(), "logcat", "-v", "time"] + (["-T", since] if since else []) + filters
  3551	    process = subprocess.Popen(args, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL, text=True)
  3552	    # A TIMER, NOT select(): a text pipe reads ahead into its own buffer, so select() on the descriptor
  3553	    # can say "nothing new" while a whole line already sits unread in Python (the first run of this
  3554	    # missed the owner's live line that way). Killing the reader from a timer makes the blocking line
  3555	    # iterator return, and the bound stays a bound.
  3556	    timer = threading.Timer(seconds, process.kill)
  3557	    timer.start()
  3558	    try:
  3559	        for line in process.stdout:
  3560	            for marker in markers:
  3561	                if marker in line:
  3562	                    return marker
  3563	        return None
  3564	    finally:
  3565	        timer.cancel()
  3566	        process.kill()
  3567	        process.wait(timeout=5)
  3568	
  3569	
  3570	def _wait_for_the_take_to_finish(seconds=90):
  3571	    """Block until the dictation reaches an ending, and say which one. None on the bound."""
  3572	    return _stream_log_until(_TERMINAL_LINES + ("insertion api=",), seconds,
  3573	                             ("DictationSession", "PasteService", "AsrService"))
  3574	
  3575	
  3576	def _wait_for_owner_listening(seconds=20):
  3577	    """Block until the session owner says the take is live, the signal that the microphone is ready.
  3578	
  3579	    The owner writes `Recording started (live after ...)` once the capture process has admitted and
  3580	    written its first audio block; the capture process's own `recording_start` comes earlier and is not
  3581	    readiness (#161, grounded round 2). A timeout is a refusal about staging, never a verdict.
  3582	    """
  3583	    marker = _stream_log_until(("Recording started (live after",), seconds, ("DictationSession",))
  3584	    if marker is None:
  3585	        raise Blocked(f"the owner never said the take went live within {seconds} s, so no audio was "
  3586	                      "fed; the microphone was never ready for it")
