codex
## Findings

1. **REPRODUCIBLE:** `SideButtonRun` can leave an instrumentation-started take running. After `VoiceInputActivity` starts the take, a LISTENING timeout, focus-staging failure, or audio-delivery failure reaches `finally`, which only closes the audio source; it neither cancels the take nor unregisters the phase listener (`VoicePipelineDeviceTest.kt:342-372`). The harness journal cannot rescue it because this take was not started through `open_recorder`.

   Drop-in fix: wrap the post-start body in `catch`; on any exception before STOP is successfully sent, send `EXTRA_CANCEL`, await IDLE with a cleanup bound, unregister the listener, then rethrow. Keep the current listener ownership only after a successful STOP.

2. **REPRODUCIBLE:** `run_device_test(timeout=…)` can hang forever without output. The deadline is checked only after `_instrumentation_groups(process.stdout)` yields (`wispr_eyes.py:2801-2806`), but that iterator blocks when instrumentation emits nothing.

   Drop-in fix: start a `threading.Timer(timeout, process.kill)` immediately after `Popen`, track whether it fired, cancel it in `finally`, and report `ISSUE: instrumentation timed out; UNKNOWN` after the stream closes. The timeout must not depend on another group arriving.

3. **HYPOTHETICAL:** Rig readiness can be satisfied by a stale receipt from a prior run. `startRig` launches the activity and waits only for an already-known filename to exist (`VoicePipelineDeviceTest.kt:509-524`). The activity writes that file before requesting focus (`PasteTargetActivity.java:97-125`), so even a current file does not prove A is focused.

   Drop-in fix: delete A/B receipts before launch and pass a random `rig_token`. Have the activity write a separate ready receipt containing that token only after `A.requestFocus()` and `A.hasFocus()` succeed. Wait for the exact token, not mere file existence.

4. **REPRODUCIBLE:** T4 excludes every group-summary notification, not only the named OS silent-section summary (`VoicePipelineDeviceTest.kt:294-304`). A future product fallback implemented as a group summary would be filtered out and the product-outcome row would falsely pass.

   Drop-in fix: replace the blanket flag filter with the exact exception: exclude only `id == 1 && tag == "g:Aggregate_SilentSection" && FLAG_GROUP_SUMMARY != 0`; keep every other group summary in the verdict.

5. **REPRODUCIBLE:** `_wait_for_the_take_to_finish` still accepts pre-insertion markers. It passes `_TERMINAL_LINES`, which includes `Auto-insert handed` and `Take terminal:` (`wispr_eyes.py:3522-3538,3570-3573`). `COMPLETED` is committed before handoff/insertion, so the wait can return before `insertion api=` exists, recreating the early-completion gap the plan says was removed.

   Drop-in fix: return on `insertion api=`, `Take ended:`, or a non-`COMPLETED` `Take terminal:` line. Explicitly ignore `Auto-insert handed` and `Take terminal: COMPLETED`; those precede the accessibility service’s outcome.

6. **REPRODUCIBLE:** Skipped instrumentation rows can look successful. Codes `-3/-4` produce only a NOTE (`wispr_eyes.py:2832-2854`), while `_print_report` treats only `ISSUE` and `BLOCKED` as non-success and prints `OK … done` otherwise (`wispr_eyes.py:3775-3788`). The same bug makes recording-off `NOT RUN` reports exit successfully.

   Drop-in fix: emit `NOT RUN:` for `-3/-4`; treat `NOT RUN` as non-success in `_print_report` and every command-result classifier. A skipped or recording-disabled row must never print `OK` or exit zero.

7. **HYPOTHETICAL:** Conflicting legacy debts for two transports of one phone are resolved by current `adb devices` order. `_migrate_locked` prepends each moved transport to the identity book (`wispr_eyes.py:232-253`), so the last enumerated alias wins for the same setting. The test explicitly accepts either conflicting timeout value (`test_wispr_eyes.py:726-735`), despite claiming first-value preservation.

   Drop-in fix: during migration, group entries by hardware identity and `_debt_key`. Identical duplicates may collapse; conflicting previous values must raise `Blocked` and leave every original key untouched for manual adjudication. Make the test require that refusal instead of accepting either value.

8. **REPRODUCIBLE:** `screen_recording` can settle an empty or invalid artifact. The `stat` status and size are ignored, then a successful pull is enough to delete the device file and settle the debt (`wispr_eyes.py:3094-3101`).

   Drop-in fix: require successful `stat`, a positive numeric size, successful pull, and a host file of the same positive size before device deletion or settlement. On failure, report the retained device path and debt.

9. **REPRODUCIBLE:** Gmail callers still preregister a plain trailing space even though the live run established Gmail stores it as U+00A0 (`docs/audits/2026-09-22-161-emulator-pass.md:8-24`). The stale literals remain in `benchmark_front_door.py:38-40,352,360`, `force_stop_scene.py:57`, and `wedge_scenes.py:97`, so their new whole-text gates will reject the known-correct Gmail result.

   Drop-in fix: use `\u00a0` for the Gmail whole-final literals in those callers. Keep plain space only for the native `PasteTargetActivity` rig.

10. **REPRODUCIBLE:** Operational documentation still names removed or changed contracts:

   - Removed test method in `.claude/knowledge/phone-audio-playback.md:64-71` and `.claude/knowledge/device-testing.md:104`.
   - Restore book still documented as keyed by transport serial at `.claude/knowledge/device-testing.md:200-204`.
   - Skill shell form still shows optional/missing required arguments at `.claude/skills/wispr-eyes/SKILL.md:100`.
   - Plan still names deleted `PasteTargetActivity.kt` at `issue-161-2026-09-22-harness-true-verdicts.md:43,85,172`.
   - The Java rig still says focus moving to B must land in A, contradicting the built fail-safe row (`PasteTargetActivity.java:34-38`).
   - H10 says `phone-audio-playback.md` becomes a pointer, but the file remains an operational speaker guide (`plan:79,105`; `phone-audio-playback.md:12-81`).

   Drop-in fix: update the method/path/key/signatures and Java comment to the built contracts. Amend H10’s AS BUILT note to say the speaker guide remains for its four retained consumers rather than claiming it became only a pointer.

## Axes (A1..A8 found/not found)

- A1: **FOUND.** All production book accesses route through `host` or `_scope_key`, and restore selects a verified transport for each identity; conflicting alias debts are nevertheless order-dependent.
- A2: **NOT FOUND.** Buffered lines can still be consumed after timer kill; `Popen.kill()` first polls and is a no-op for an already-dead POSIX child. `_STATE` is process-local, so another harness process cannot switch this process’s selected device.
- A3: **FOUND.** Lock ownership and PID-exit handling are safe; artifact validation is incomplete. Pull failure keeps the debt/file, and later restore’s deletion is the documented cleanup policy.
- A4: **FOUND.** Class syntax, READY grouping, rebind/restore, and `0/-1/-2` classification are sound. The silent-stream timeout and `-3/-4`/`NOT RUN` classification are not.
- A5: **FOUND.** `runBlocking`/Room, consistent UiAutomation flags, and ordered-broadcast threading do not deadlock. Take cleanup, rig freshness/focus, and notification filtering have gaps.
- A6: **NOT FOUND.** The rig is Java-only, registers exported on API 33+, answers an ordered broadcast legally, unregisters, and clears its static field.
- A7: **FOUND.** `dictate-once.sh` depends only on retained components, but live docs still point to the deleted launcher test.
- A8: **FOUND.** The early terminal wait, Gmail literals, retained speaker guide, Java conversion, and documentation differ from promises or AS BUILT wording.

## VERDICT: FIX-THEN-RERUN

The harness journal cannot rescue it because this take was not started through `open_recorder`.

   Drop-in fix: wrap the post-start body in `catch`; on any exception before STOP is successfully sent, send `EXTRA_CANCEL`, await IDLE with a cleanup bound, unregister the listener, then rethrow. Keep the current listener ownership only after a successful STOP.

2. **REPRODUCIBLE:** `run_device_test(timeout=…)` can hang forever without output. The deadline is checked only after `_instrumentation_groups(process.stdout)` yields (`wispr_eyes.py:2801-2806`), but that iterator blocks when instrumentation emits nothing.

   Drop-in fix: start a `threading.Timer(timeout, process.kill)` immediately after `Popen`, track whether it fired, cancel it in `finally`, and report `ISSUE: instrumentation timed out; UNKNOWN` after the stream closes. The timeout must not depend on another group arriving.

3. **HYPOTHETICAL:** Rig readiness can be satisfied by a stale receipt from a prior run. `startRig` launches the activity and waits only for an already-known filename to exist (`VoicePipelineDeviceTest.kt:509-524`). The activity writes that file before requesting focus (`PasteTargetActivity.java:97-125`), so even a current file does not prove A is focused.

   Drop-in fix: delete A/B receipts before launch and pass a random `rig_token`. Have the activity write a separate ready receipt containing that token only after `A.requestFocus()` and `A.hasFocus()` succeed. Wait for the exact token, not mere file existence.

4. **REPRODUCIBLE:** T4 excludes every group-summary notification, not only the named OS silent-section summary (`VoicePipelineDeviceTest.kt:294-304`). A future product fallback implemented as a group summary would be filtered out and the product-outcome row would falsely pass.

   Drop-in fix: replace the blanket flag filter with the exact exception: exclude only `id == 1 && tag == "g:Aggregate_SilentSection" && FLAG_GROUP_SUMMARY != 0`; keep every other group summary in the verdict.

5. **REPRODUCIBLE:** `_wait_for_the_take_to_finish` still accepts pre-insertion markers. It passes `_TERMINAL_LINES`, which includes `Auto-insert handed` and `Take terminal:` (`wispr_eyes.py:3522-3538,3570-3573`). `COMPLETED` is committed before handoff/insertion, so the wait can return before `insertion api=` exists, recreating the early-completion gap the plan says was removed.

   Drop-in fix: return on `insertion api=`, `Take ended:`, or a non-`COMPLETED` `Take terminal:` line. Explicitly ignore `Auto-insert handed` and `Take terminal: COMPLETED`; those precede the accessibility service’s outcome.

6. **REPRODUCIBLE:** Skipped instrumentation rows can look successful. Codes `-3/-4` produce only a NOTE (`wispr_eyes.py:2832-2854`), while `_print_report` treats only `ISSUE` and `BLOCKED` as non-success and prints `OK … done` otherwise (`wispr_eyes.py:3775-3788`). The same bug makes recording-off `NOT RUN` reports exit successfully.

   Drop-in fix: emit `NOT RUN:` for `-3/-4`; treat `NOT RUN` as non-success in `_print_report` and every command-result classifier. A skipped or recording-disabled row must never print `OK` or exit zero.

7. **HYPOTHETICAL:** Conflicting legacy debts for two transports of one phone are resolved by current `adb devices` order. `_migrate_locked` prepends each moved transport to the identity book (`wispr_eyes.py:232-253`), so the last enumerated alias wins for the same setting. The test explicitly accepts either conflicting timeout value (`test_wispr_eyes.py:726-735`), despite claiming first-value preservation.

   Drop-in fix: during migration, group entries by hardware identity and `_debt_key`. Identical duplicates may collapse; conflicting previous values must raise `Blocked` and leave every original key untouched for manual adjudication. Make the test require that refusal instead of accepting either value.

8. **REPRODUCIBLE:** `screen_recording` can settle an empty or invalid artifact. The `stat` status and size are ignored, then a successful pull is enough to delete the device file and settle the debt (`wispr_eyes.py:3094-3101`).

   Drop-in fix: require successful `stat`, a positive numeric size, successful pull, and a host file of the same positive size before device deletion or settlement. On failure, report the retained device path and debt.

9. **REPRODUCIBLE:** Gmail callers still preregister a plain trailing space even though the live run established Gmail stores it as U+00A0 (`docs/audits/2026-09-22-161-emulator-pass.md:8-24`). The stale literals remain in `benchmark_front_door.py:38-40,352,360`, `force_stop_scene.py:57`, and `wedge_scenes.py:97`, so their new whole-text gates will reject the known-correct Gmail result.

   Drop-in fix: use `\u00a0` for the Gmail whole-final literals in those callers. Keep plain space only for the native `PasteTargetActivity` rig.

10. **REPRODUCIBLE:** Operational documentation still names removed or changed contracts:

   - Removed test method in `.claude/knowledge/phone-audio-playback.md:64-71` and `.claude/knowledge/device-testing.md:104`.
   - Restore book still documented as keyed by transport serial at `.claude/knowledge/device-testing.md:200-204`.
   - Skill shell form still shows optional/missing required arguments at `.claude/skills/wispr-eyes/SKILL.md:100`.
   - Plan still names deleted `PasteTargetActivity.kt` at `issue-161-2026-09-22-harness-true-verdicts.md:43,85,172`.
   - The Java rig still says focus moving to B must land in A, contradicting the built fail-safe row (`PasteTargetActivity.java:34-38`).
   - H10 says `phone-audio-playback.md` becomes a pointer, but the file remains an operational speaker guide (`plan:79,105`; `phone-audio-playback.md:12-81`).

   Drop-in fix: update the method/path/key/signatures and Java comment to the built contracts. Amend H10’s AS BUILT note to say the speaker guide remains for its four retained consumers rather than claiming it became only a pointer.

## Axes (A1..A8 found/not found)

- A1: **FOUND.** All production book accesses route through `host` or `_scope_key`, and restore selects a verified transport for each identity; conflicting alias debts are nevertheless order-dependent.
- A2: **NOT FOUND.** Buffered lines can still be consumed after timer kill; `Popen.kill()` first polls and is a no-op for an already-dead POSIX child. `_STATE` is process-local, so another harness process cannot switch this process’s selected device.
- A3: **FOUND.** Lock ownership and PID-exit handling are safe; artifact validation is incomplete. Pull failure keeps the debt/file, and later restore’s deletion is the documented cleanup policy.
- A4: **FOUND.** Class syntax, READY grouping, rebind/restore, and `0/-1/-2` classification are sound. The silent-stream timeout and `-3/-4`/`NOT RUN` classification are not.
- A5: **FOUND.** `runBlocking`/Room, consistent UiAutomation flags, and ordered-broadcast threading do not deadlock. Take cleanup, rig freshness/focus, and notification filtering have gaps.
- A6: **NOT FOUND.** The rig is Java-only, registers exported on API 33+, answers an ordered broadcast legally, unregisters, and clears its static field.
- A7: **FOUND.** `dictate-once.sh` depends only on retained components, but live docs still point to the deleted launcher test.
- A8: **FOUND.** The early terminal wait, Gmail literals, retained speaker guide, Java conversion, and documentation differ from promises or AS BUILT wording.

## VERDICT: FIX-THEN-RERUN
